## HT-NEXT-267 — 2026-11-05 Organization-adaptive cross-domain study (plan only)

Status: `STAGE1_GATE_PASS_LEARNABLE_HEADROOM_FAIL_TEST_NOT_OPENED` (Stage 1 audit: `../docs/healthtech/audits/ptp-org-adaptive-stage1-20261105/README.md`; Stage 0 audit: `../docs/healthtech/audits/ptp-org-adaptive-stage0-20261105/README.md`). Protocol draft: `../docs/healthtech/research/ORG_ADAPTIVE_CROSS_DOMAIN_PROTOCOL_DRAFT_20261105_EN.md`. PoC plan: `../docs/healthtech/research/ORG_ADAPTIVE_POC_PLAN_20261105_EN.md`.

- Target: adaptive, cost-accounted candidate acquisition for DAPFAM strict-OUT exposure at an equal budget of 200 families. Qwen3-8B actions (functional abstraction, IPC-shift, pseudo-document), a contextual-bandit policy, and simulated organizations defined by IPC section A–H.
- Owner decisions (2026-11-05):
  - The study is written as a new self-contained manuscript. Earlier unpublished analyses are superseded and not cited.
  - Use only the neutral terms in `../docs/healthtech/research/ORG_ADAPTIVE_MANUSCRIPT_PLAN_20261105_EN.md`.
  - Organizations are IPC sections.
  - The fixed baseline is the strongest dense passage retriever (PatEmbed-large passages, single-window or multi-window query).
- Stage 0 outcomes:
  - The internal consistency check passed with delta 0.
  - Multi-window queries were worse than single-window on dev, a negative result; R2 stays.
  - Qwen prompt v1 passed the canary (1 of 40 calls failed).
- Stage 1 outcomes (dev):
  - The preregistered gate passed.
  - M\* is R2 alone.
  - The cross-fitted global mix and the per-section mix both fell below R2 (Amendment 1). The fixed-mix and organization-adaptive claims are not carried forward.
  - Q actions reach 120 strict-OUT positives that no R action reaches.
- Next: owner decision on direction. Do not open test before a Stage 2 freeze commit. No paid calls.
- Solution-scout feasibility (dev, 6 Nov 2026): `PARTIAL_LANE_ONLY` ([audit](../docs/healthtech/audits/ptp-solution-scout-feasibility-20261105/README.md); [plan](../docs/healthtech/research/SOLUTION_SCOUT_FEASIBILITY_PLAN_20261105_EN.md), pre-declared at `3c4294e`).

Installed an isolated validation environment with `torch==2.8.0+cpu`, `transformers==4.51.3`, `safetensors==0.5.3`, and `accelerate==1.6.0`. Offline Qwen3 config/tokenizer loading passed with `trust_remote_code=false`. Full Windows weight loading was not attempted because the host snapshot has `16,824,262,656` RAM bytes and no CUDA; this is a runtime boundary, not a model-integrity failure. Known local setup plus a future remote Qwen copy is estimated at `38,186,418,222` bytes against the `42,949,672,960` hard bound, leaving `4,763,254,738` bytes before any additional remote runtime/storage overhead.

O3 remains blocked on canonical cumulative ledger identity, current account credit, exact comparison authorization, rights-cleared outbound dossier prompts, and remote-V100 local-generation compatibility. Next: inspect remote storage/allocation, transfer only the pinned model if the measured peak remains within the bound, run a bounded non-thinking local-generation canary, and keep the API branch closed until all paid gates are evidenced.
